.claude\rules\layered-gates-architecture.md:78:Cite rationale: this matches the natural structure of Claude Code's hook event lifecycle per https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks (verbatim "PreToolUse: Before a tool call executes. Can block it" / "PostToolUse: After a tool call succeeds. No (post-execution)" / "Stop: When Claude finishes responding. Can block it"). Each event has a fundamentally different role in the lifecycle; conflating them produces design errors.

.claude\rules\layered-gates-architecture.md:209:**T4 naming clarification** (drift fix in this session's Ship #1): the script name `codex_prepush_review.py` is historical — the wire is PostToolUse `Bash(git push *)` so it fires AFTER push completes.  201: def main() -> int:
 202:     if os.environ.get("CODEX_STUCK_DETECTOR_DISABLE") == "1":
 203:         return 0
 204: 
 205:     threshold_min = int(os.environ.get("CODEX_STUCK_THRESHOLD_MIN", "10"))
 206:     min_bytes = int(os.environ.get("CODEX_STUCK_MIN_OUTPUT_BYTES", "500"))
 207: 
 208:     _clean_orphan_streams()
 209: 
 210:     with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
 211:         sys.stdin.read()
 212: 
 213:     rows = _read_jsonl_rows()
 214:     if not rows:
 215:         return 0
 216: 
 217:     now = time.time()
 218:     latest = _latest_launch_by_pid(rows)
 219:     killed_count = 0
 220: 
 221:     for pid, (src, row) in latest.items():
 222:         if row.get("status") != "launched":
 223:             continue
 224: 
 225:         ts_epoch = _parse_iso_ts(row.get("ts", ""))
 226:         if ts_epoch == 0:
 227:             continue
 228:         age_min = (now - ts_epoch) / 60.0
 229:         if age_min < threshold_min:
 230:             continue
 231: 
 232:         outfile_path = row.get("outfile")
 233:         if not outfile_path:
 234:             continue
 235:         outfile = Path(outfile_path)
 236:         size = outfile.stat().st_size if outfile.exists() else 0
 237: 
 238:         if size >= min_bytes:
 239:             continue
 240: 
 241:         if not _pid_alive(pid):
 242:             continue
 243: 
 244:         killed = _kill_pid(pid)
 245:         kill_record = {
 246:             "sha": row.get("sha"),
 247:             "pid": pid,
 248:             "outfile": outfile_path,
 249:             "status": "stuck_killed" if killed else "stuck_kill_failed",
 250:             "age_min": round(age_min, 1),
 251:             # Fire 3.b instrumentation per Fire 3 codex T1 F-DUR-002 deferred scope
 252:             # (commit f995825). Sister field to `commit_duration_ms` on
 253:             # codex_postcommit/prepush_review.py launch rows; enables paired-latency
 254:             # analysis (commit-detection-to-codex-spawn vs codex-spawn-to-stuck-kill).
 255:             "launch_age_ms": int(age_min * 60_000),
 256:             "output_bytes": size,
 257:             "threshold_min": threshold_min,
 258:             "ts": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
 259:         }
 260:         try:
 261:             with src.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 262:                 f.write(json.dumps(kill_record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
 263:         except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
 264:             print(
 265:                 f"codex_stuck_detector: jsonl write failed for {src}: {e!r}",
 266:                 file=sys.stderr,
 267:             )
 268:         if killed:
 269:             killed_count += 1
 270:             print(
 271:                 f"codex_stuck_detector: killed stuck pid={pid} age={age_min:.1f}min output={size}B",
 272:                 file=sys.stderr,
 273:             )
 274: 
 275:     # Sibling thread-bridge integration: best-effort import, skip if not ported yet.
 276:     try:
 277:         sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
 278:         from codex_review_thread_bridge import bridge_unresolved_rows  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
 279: 
 280:         for log_path in _LAUNCH_LOGS:
 281:             try:
 282:                 bridge_unresolved_rows(log_path, max_rows=10)
 283:             except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
 284:                 print(
 285:                     f"codex_stuck_detector: bridge({log_path.name}) failed: {e!r}",
 286:                     file=sys.stderr,
 287:                 )
 288:     except ImportError:
 289:         # Not ported into sss yet — silent skip (port-time deferred dep).
 290:         pass
 291:     except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
 292:         print(f"codex_stuck_detector: bridge integration failed: {e!r}", file=sys.stderr)
 293: 
 294:     return 0

 226: def bridge_unresolved_rows(jsonl_path: Path, *, max_rows: int = 50) -> int:
 227:     """Walk a postcommit/prepush JSONL file and bridge unresolved threads.
 228: 
 229:     Iterates `status: launched` rows where broker_tracked=true and
 230:     (sha, pid) is not in already_resolved_keys. For each, calls
 231:     resolve_thread_id and appends the resolution if successful.
 232: 
 233:     Returns: count of newly-resolved rows.
 234: 
 235:     Bounded by max_rows to keep the Stop-hook responsive (each
 236:     resolve_thread_id call may sleep up to timeout_s polling for the
 237:     stream file). Default 50 covers typical session volume.
 238:     """
 239:     if not jsonl_path.exists():
 240:         return 0
 241:     if os.environ.get("CODEX_REVIEW_THREAD_BRIDGE_DISABLE") == "1":
 242:         return 0
 243: 
 244:     resolved_keys = already_resolved_keys(jsonl_path)
 245:     resolved_count = 0
 246: 
 247:     # Walk launched rows; skip rows whose stream_file already has a
 248:     # thread_resolved row (cycle-590 ship 17 fix-forward — was (sha, pid)
 249:     # tuple key, switched to stream_file path for stable per-launch
 250:     # identity. See already_resolved_keys() docstring for full rationale.)
 251:     for row in _iter_jsonl_rows(jsonl_path):
 252:         if resolved_count >= max_rows:
 253:             break
 254:         if row.get("status") != "launched":
 255:             continue
 256:         if row.get("broker_tracked") is not True:
 257:             continue
 258:         sha = row.get("sha", "")
 259:         if not isinstance(sha, str) or not sha:
 260:             continue
 261:         sf = row.get("stream_file", "")
 262:         if not isinstance(sf, str) or not sf:
 263:             # Pre-ship-17 launched rows lacked stream_file; cannot dedup
 264:             # safely. Skip — they'd otherwise re-resolve every fire and
 265:             # potentially write phantom thread_resolved rows.
 266:             continue
 267:         key = sf
 268:         if key in resolved_keys:
 269:             continue
 270:         # Use shorter timeout in batch mode — fast-path on healthy fires
 271:         # where stream file should already exist by the time the Stop-hook
 272:         # iterates (postcommit_review fires first, then turn completes,
 273:         # then Stop-hook picks up the trail).
 274:         resolution = resolve_thread_id(
 275:             row,
 276:             timeout_s=2.0,  # bounded — Stop-hook can't block long
 277:             poll_interval_s=0.25,
 278:         )
 279:         if resolution is None:
 280:             continue
 281:         if append_resolution(jsonl_path, resolution):
 282:             resolved_keys.add(key)
 283:             resolved_count += 1
 284: 
 285:     return resolved_count
 286: 
 287: 
 288: def main() -> int:
 289:     """Stand-alone invocation: bridge both postcommit + prepush JSONL files.
 290: 
 291:     Useful for ad-hoc invocation or as a SessionStart consumer. Stop-hook
 292:     wiring is via codex_stuck_detector.py importing bridge_unresolved_rows.
 293:     """
 294:     if os.environ.get("CODEX_REVIEW_THREAD_BRIDGE_DISABLE") == "1":
 295:         return 0
 296:     total = 0
 297:     for path in (_POSTCOMMIT_LOG, _PREPUSH_LOG):
 298:         total += bridge_unresolved_rows(path)
 299:     if total > 0:
 300:         # Surface to stderr for SessionStart additionalContext when wired
 301:         print(
 302:             f"codex_review_thread_bridge: resolved {total} thread_id mappings",
 303:             file=__import__("sys").stderr,
 304:         )
 305:     return 0
 306: 
 307: 
 308: if __name__ == "__main__":
 309:     raise SystemExit(main())

**Considering commit processes**

I'm thinking about how commit-on-stop-throttled tracks changes. It seems like the codex_stop_review_gate reviews the worktree, including dirty, staged, and untracked files, before closing the session. If it runs after a successful commit, it could end up reviewing nothing since the changes have already been committed. That means I might lose a review of those modified files. This process feels a bit confusing, and I need to keep that in mind while working!
**Evaluating commit behavior**

Also, I wonder if the throttled version only commits existing tracked changes and skips committing if there are none explicit. So, I should probably read up on this!

  14: import { resolveWorkspaceRoot } from "./lib/workspace.mjs";
  15: 
  16: const STOP_REVIEW_TIMEOUT_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
  17: const SCRIPT_DIR = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  18: const ROOT_DIR = path.resolve(SCRIPT_DIR, "..");
  19: const STOP_REVIEW_TASK_MARKER = "Run a stop-gate review of the previous Claude turn.";
  20: 
  21: function readHookInput() {
  22:   const raw = fs.readFileSync(0, "utf8").trim();
  23:   if (!raw) {
  24:     return {};
  25:   }
  26:   return JSON.parse(raw);
  27: }
  28: 
  29: function emitDecision(payload) {
  30:   process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(payload)}\n`);
  31: }
  32: 
  33: function logNote(message) {
  34:   if (!message) {
  35:     return;
  36:   }
  37:   process.stderr.write(`${message}\n`);
  38: }
  39: 
  40: function filterJobsForCurrentSession(jobs, input = {}) {
  41:   const sessionId = input.session_id || process.env[SESSION_ID_ENV] || null;
  42:   if (!sessionId) {
  43:     return jobs;
  44:   }
  45:   return jobs.filter((job) => job.sessionId === sessionId);
  46: }
  47: 
  48: function buildStopReviewPrompt(input = {}) {
  49:   const lastAssistantMessage = String(input.last_assistant_message ?? "").trim();
  50:   const template = loadPromptTemplate(ROOT_DIR, "stop-review-gate");
  51:   const claudeResponseBlock = lastAssistantMessage
  52:     ? ["Previous Claude response:", lastAssistantMessage].join("\n")
  53:     : "";
  54:   return interpolateTemplate(template, {
  55:     CLAUDE_RESPONSE_BLOCK: claudeResponseBlock
  56:   });
  57: }
  58: 
  59: function buildSetupNote(cwd) {
  60:   const availability = getCodexAvailability(cwd);
  61:   if (availability.available) {
  62:     return null;
  63:   }
  64: 
  65:   const detail = availability.detail ? ` ${availability.detail}.` : "";
  66:   return `Codex is not set up for the review gate.${detail} Run /codex:setup.`;
  67: }
  68: 
  69: function parseStopReviewOutput(rawOutput) {
  70:   const text = String(rawOutput ?? "").trim();
  71:   if (!text) {
  72:     return {
  73:       ok: false,
  74:       reason:
  75:         "The stop-time Codex review task returned no final output. Run /codex:review --wait manually or bypass the gate."
  76:     };
  77:   }
  78: 
  79:   const firstLine = text.split(/\r?\n/, 1)[0].trim();
  80:   if (firstLine.startsWith("ALLOW:")) {
  81:     return { ok: true, reason: null };
  82:   }
  83:   if (firstLine.startsWith("BLOCK:")) {
  84:     const reason = firstLine.slice("BLOCK:".length).trim() || text;
  85:     return {
  86:       ok: false,
  87:       reason: `Codex stop-time review found issues that still need fixes before ending the session: ${reason}`
  88:     };
  89:   }
  90: 
  91:   return {
  92:     ok: false,
  93:     reason:
  94:       "The stop-time Codex review task returned an unexpected answer. Run /codex:review --wait manually or bypass the gate."
  95:   };
  96: }
  97: 
  98: function runStopReview(cwd, input = {}) {
  99:   const scriptPath = path.join(SCRIPT_DIR, "codex-companion.mjs");
 100:   const prompt = buildStopReviewPrompt(input);
 101:   const childEnv = {
 102:     ...process.env,
 103:     ...(input.session_id ? { [SESSION_ID_ENV]: input.session_id } : {})
 104:   };
 105:   const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [scriptPath, "task", "--json", prompt], {
 106:     cwd,
 107:     env: childEnv,
 108:     encoding: "utf8",
 109:     timeout: STOP_REVIEW_TIMEOUT_MS
 110:   });
 111: 
 112:   if (result.error?.code === "ETIMEDOUT") {
 113:     return {
 114:       ok: false,
 115:       reason:
 116:         "The stop-time Codex review task timed out after 15 minutes. Run /codex:review --wait manually or bypass the gate."
 117:     };
 118:   }
 119: 
 120:   if (result.status !== 0) {
 121:     const detail = String(result.stderr || result.stdout || "").trim();
 122:     return {
 123:       ok: false,
 124:       reason: detail
 125:         ? `The stop-time Codex review task failed: ${detail}`
 126:         : "The stop-time Codex review task failed. Run /codex:review --wait manually or bypass the gate."
 127:     };
 128:   }
 129: 
 130:   try {
 131:     const payload = JSON.parse(result.stdout);
 132:     return parseStopReviewOutput(payload?.rawOutput);
 133:   } catch {
 134:     return {
 135:       ok: false,
 136:       reason:
 137:         "The stop-time Codex review task returned invalid JSON. Run /codex:review --wait manually or bypass the gate."
 138:     };
 139:   }
 140: }
 141: 
 142: function main() {
 143:   const input = readHookInput();
 144:   const cwd = input.cwd || process.env.CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR || process.cwd();
 145:   const workspaceRoot = resolveWorkspaceRoot(cwd);
 146:   const config = getConfig(workspaceRoot);
 147: 
 148:   const jobs = sortJobsNewestFirst(filterJobsForCurrentSession(listJobs(workspaceRoot), input));
 149:   const runningJob = jobs.find((job) => job.status === "queued" || job.status === "running");
 150:   const runningTaskNote = runningJob
 151:     ? `Codex task ${runningJob.id} is still running. Check /codex:status and use /codex:cancel ${runningJob.id} if you want to stop it before ending the session.`
 152:     : null;
 153: 
 154:   if (!config.stopReviewGate) {
 155:     logNote(runningTaskNote);
 156:     return;
 157:   }
 158: 
 159:   const setupNote = buildSetupNote(cwd);
 160:   if (setupNote) {
 161:     logNote(setupNote);
 162:     logNote(runningTaskNote);
 163:     return;
 164:   }
 165: 
 166:   const review = runStopReview(cwd, input);
 167:   if (!review.ok) {
 168:     emitDecision({
 169:       decision: "block",
 170:       reason: runningTaskNote ? `${runningTaskNote} ${review.reason}` : review.reason
 171:     });
 172:     return;
 173:   }
 174: 
 175:   logNote(runningTaskNote);
 176: }
 177: 
 178: try {
 179:   main();
 180: } catch (error) {
 181:   const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
 182:   process.stderr.write(`${message}\n`);
 183:   process.exitCode = 1;
 184: }

**Evaluating execution order**

I’m thinking about the async process here. If I launch something before A5, it might tree-kill old codex jobs without blocking anything, which sounds good. But the user mentioned stopping the 5th after A5. I realize that if A5 consumes codex resources, it would be best to clean up the old stuck jobs first to avoid complications. It’s all about ensuring smooth operations, which could get tricky!

   1: import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
   2: import fs from "node:fs";
   3: import os from "node:os";

   6: import { resolveWorkspaceRoot } from "./workspace.mjs";

   8: const STATE_VERSION = 1;
   9: const PLUGIN_DATA_ENV = "CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA";
  10: const FALLBACK_STATE_ROOT_DIR = path.join(os.tmpdir(), "codex-companion");
  11: const STATE_FILE_NAME = "state.json";
  12: const JOBS_DIR_NAME = "jobs";
  13: const MAX_JOBS = 50;
  14: 
  15: function nowIso() {
  16:   return new Date().toISOString();
  17: }
  18: 
  19: function defaultState() {
  20:   return {
  21:     version: STATE_VERSION,
  22:     config: {
  23:       stopReviewGate: false
  24:     },
  25:     jobs: []
  26:   };
  27: }
  28: 
  29: export function resolveStateDir(cwd) {
  30:   const workspaceRoot = resolveWorkspaceRoot(cwd);
  31:   let canonicalWorkspaceRoot = workspaceRoot;
  32:   try {
  33:     canonicalWorkspaceRoot = fs.realpathSync.native(workspaceRoot);
  34:   } catch {
  35:     canonicalWorkspaceRoot = workspaceRoot;
  36:   }
  37: 
  38:   const slugSource = path.basename(workspaceRoot) || "workspace";
  39:   const slug = slugSource.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "") || "workspace";
  40:   const hash = createHash("sha256").update(canonicalWorkspaceRoot).digest("hex").slice(0, 16);
  41:   const pluginDataDir = process.env[PLUGIN_DATA_ENV];
  42:   const stateRoot = pluginDataDir ? path.join(pluginDataDir, "state") : FALLBACK_STATE_ROOT_DIR;
  43:   return path.join(stateRoot, `${slug}-${hash}`);
  44: }
  45: 
  46: export function resolveStateFile(cwd) {
  47:   return path.join(resolveStateDir(cwd), STATE_FILE_NAME);
  48: }
  49: 
  50: export function resolveJobsDir(cwd) {
  51:   return path.join(resolveStateDir(cwd), JOBS_DIR_NAME);
  52: }
  53: 
  54: export function ensureStateDir(cwd) {
  55:   fs.mkdirSync(resolveJobsDir(cwd), { recursive: true });
  56: }
  57: 
  58: export function loadState(cwd) {
  59:   const stateFile = resolveStateFile(cwd);
  60:   if (!fs.existsSync(stateFile)) {
  61:     return defaultState();
  62:   }
  63: 
  64:   try {
  65:     const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(stateFile, "utf8"));
  66:     return {
  67:       ...defaultState(),
  68:       ...parsed,
  69:       config: {
  70:         ...defaultState().config,
  71:         ...(parsed.config ?? {})
  72:       },
  73:       jobs: Array.isArray(parsed.jobs) ? parsed.jobs : []
  74:     };
  75:   } catch {
  76:     return defaultState();
  77:   }
  78: }
  79: 
  80: function pruneJobs(jobs) {
  81:   return [...jobs]
  82:     .sort((left, right) => String(right.updatedAt ?? "").localeCompare(String(left.updatedAt ?? "")))
  83:     .slice(0, MAX_JOBS);
  84: }
  85: 
  86: function removeFileIfExists(filePath) {
  87:   if (filePath && fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
  88:     fs.unlinkSync(filePath);
  89:   }
  90: }
  91: 
  92: export function saveState(cwd, state) {
  93:   const previousJobs = loadState(cwd).jobs;
  94:   ensureStateDir(cwd);
  95:   const nextJobs = pruneJobs(state.jobs ?? []);
  96:   const nextState = {
  97:     version: STATE_VERSION,
  98:     config: {
  99:       ...defaultState().config,
 100:       ...(state.config ?? {})
 101:     },
 102:     jobs: nextJobs
 103:   };
 104: 
 105:   const retainedIds = new Set(nextJobs.map((job) => job.id));
 106:   for (const job of previousJobs) {
 107:     if (retainedIds.has(job.id)) {
 108:       continue;
 109:     }
 110:     removeJobFile(resolveJobFile(cwd, job.id));
 111:     removeFileIfExists(job.logFile);
 112:   }
 113: 
 114:   fs.writeFileSync(resolveStateFile(cwd), `${JSON.stringify(nextState, null, 2)}\n`, "utf8");
 115:   return nextState;
 116: }
 117: 
 118: export function updateState(cwd, mutate) {
 119:   const state = loadState(cwd);
 120:   mutate(state);
 121:   return saveState(cwd, state);
 122: }
 123: 
 124: export function generateJobId(prefix = "job") {
 125:   const random = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
 126:   return `${prefix}-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${random}`;
 127: }
